3	<div class="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">
4	  <div class="mb-6">
5	    <h1 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">Create Sandcastle</h1>
6	    <p class="text-gray-500 mt-1">Configure and create a new sandbox environment</p>
7	  </div>
8	
9	  <%= form_with url: sandboxes_path, method: :post, class: "space-y-6" do |form| %>
10	    <%# Basic Information %>
11	    <div class="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-6 space-y-4">
12	      <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Basic Information</h2>
13	
14	      <div>
15	        <%= form.label :name, "Sandbox Name", class: "block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1" %>
16	        <%= form.text_field :name,
17	              required: true,
18	              pattern: "[a-z][a-z0-9_\\-]*",
19	              maxlength: 63,
20	              placeholder: "",
21	              id: "sandbox_name",
22	              class: "w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500" %>
23	        <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">Lowercase letters, numbers, hyphens, and underscores only</p>
24	      </div>
25	
26	      <div>
27	        <%= form.label :image, "Container Image", class: "block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1" %>
28	        <%= form.text_field :image,
29	              placeholder: "ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest",
30	              class: "w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500" %>
31	        <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">Leave empty to use the default image</p>
32	      </div>
33	    </div>
34	
35	    <%# Restore from Snapshot %>
36	    <% if @snapshots.any? %>
37	      <div class="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-6 space-y-4">
38	        <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Restore from Snapshot</h2>
39	
40	        <div>
41	          <%= form.label :snapshot, "Snapshot", class: "block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1" %>
42	          <%= form.select :snapshot,
43	                options_for_select(
44	                  [["None (fresh sandbox)", ""]] +
45	                  @snapshots.map { |s|
46	                    label = s[:name]
47	                    label += " — #{s[:label]}" if s[:label].present?
48	                    label += " [#{s[:layers].join(', ')}]" if s[:layers].present?
49	                    label += " (from #{s[:source_sandbox]})" if s[:source_sandbox].present?
50	                    [label, s[:name]]
51	                  },
52	                  params[:snapshot]
53	                ),
54	                {},
55	                class: "w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500" %>
56	          <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
57	            Create a new sandbox from an existing snapshot.
58	            <% unless @btrfs_available %>
59	              (Only container layer available — BTRFS not detected)
60	            <% end %>
61	          </p>
62	        </div>
63	      </div>
64	    <% end %>
65	
66	    <%# Storage Options %>
67	    <div class="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-6 space-y-4">
68	      <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Storage Options</h2>
69	
70	      <div class="space-y-3">
71	        <label class="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer">
72	          <%= form.check_box :mount_home, checked: @defaults.default_mount_home, class: "mt-1 h-4 w-4 text-blue-600 border-gray-300 rounded focus:ring-blue-500" %>
73	          <div class="flex-1">
74	            <div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">Mount persistent home directory</div>
75	            <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Your home directory (~/) will persist across all sandboxes</p>
76	          </div>
77	        </label>
78	
79	        <label class="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer">
80	          <%= form.check_box :mount_data, id: "sandbox_mount_data", checked: @defaults.default_data_path.present?,
81	                class: "mt-1 h-4 w-4 text-blue-600 border-gray-300 rounded focus:ring-blue-500" %>
82	          <div class="flex-1">
83	            <div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">Mount /persisted</div>
84	            <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Mount a persistent data volume at /persisted in the sandbox</p>
85	          </div>
86	        </label>
87	
88	        <div id="data_path_row">
89	          <%= form.label :data_path, "Data Subpath (optional)", class: "block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1" %>
90	          <%= form.text_field :data_path,
91	                value: @defaults.default_data_path.presence&.then { |p| p == "." ? "" : p },

93	                class: "w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500" %>
94	          <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">Leave empty for the root data directory, or specify a subpath</p>
95	        </div>
96	      </div>
97	    </div>
98

106	          <div class="flex-1">
107	            <div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">Connect to Tailscale network</div>
108	            <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Automatically connect this sandbox to your Tailscale network</p>
109	          </div>
110	        </label>
111	      </div>
112	    <% end %>
113	
114	    <%# Services %>
115	    <div class="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-6 space-y-4">
116	      <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Services</h2>
117	
118	      <label class="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer">
119	        <%= form.check_box :docker_enabled, checked: @defaults.default_docker_enabled,
120	              class: "mt-1 h-4 w-4 text-blue-600 border-gray-300 rounded focus:ring-blue-500" %>
121	        <div class="flex-1">
122	          <div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">Start Docker daemon</div>
123	          <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Enable Docker-in-Docker support inside the sandbox</p>
124	        </div>
125	      </label>
126	
127	      <label class="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer">
128	        <%= form.check_box :ssh_start_tmux, checked: @defaults.default_ssh_start_tmux,

130	        <div class="flex-1">

135	
136	      <label class="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer">

140	        <div class="flex-1">
141	          <div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">Enable VNC display</div>
142	          <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Start a virtual display (Xvnc) for GUI apps and browser access</p>

145	
146	      <% smb_ready = Current.user.tailscale_enabled? && Current.user.smb_password.present? %>
147	      <label class="flex items-start gap-3 <%= smb_ready ? 'cursor-pointer' : 'opacity-50' %>">
148	        <%= form.check_box :smb_enabled,

150	              title: smb_ready ? nil : "Requires Tailscale enabled and an SMB password set in Settings",

{
  "command": "mkdir -p /tmp/tmux-test && cd /tmp/tmux-test && cat > bashrc-start <<'EOF'\n# ~/.bashrc: executed by bash(1) for non-login shells.\n\n# If not running interactively, don't do anything\ncase $- in\n    *i*) ;;\n      *) return;;\nesac\n\nHISTCONTROL=ignoreboth\nHISTSIZE=1000\n\n# some aliases\nalias ll='ls -la'\nEOF\n\n# Now simulate what entrypoint.sh does\nBASHRC=/tmp/tmux-test/bashrc-start\nBEGIN_MARK=\"# >>> sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach >>>\"\nEND_MARK=\"# <<< sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach <<<\"\n\nsed -i \"/^${BEGIN_MARK}$/,/^${END_MARK}$/d\" \"$BASHRC\"\n\nSANDCASTLE_SSH_START_TMUX=1\nif [ \"${SANDCASTLE_SSH_START_TMUX:-1}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n    cat >> \"$BASHRC\" <<'TMUX_EOF'\n# >>> sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach >>>\n# Auto-attach to a shared tmux session for interactive SSH logins.\nif [ -z \"$TMUX\" ] && [ -z \"$NO_TMUX\" ] && [ -n \"$SSH_CONNECTION\" ] && [[ $- == *i* ]] && command -v tmux >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    exec tmux new-session -A -s main\nfi\n# <<< sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach <<<\nTMUX_EOF\nfi\n\necho \"=== Result .bashrc ===\"\ncat \"$BASHRC\"\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Second run (idempotency test) ===\"\nsed -i \"/^${BEGIN_MARK}$/,/^${END_MARK}$/d\" \"$BASHRC\"\ncat \"$BASHRC\"",
  "description": "Test entrypoint.sh tmux block logic"
}

> AGENT

**Bug found.** The tmux auto-attach block is appended to `~/.bashrc`, but SSH login shells only read `~/.bashrc` if `~/.profile` (or `~/.bash_profile`) exists and sources it. With `mount_home=true` the home dir is bind-mounted and `useradd -m` skips copying `/etc/skel/*`, so there's no `.profile` → `.bashrc` never runs on SSH login → tmux never starts.

Fix: move the block to `/etc/profile.d/sandcastle-tmux.sh` (always sourced by login shells, regardless of user's home contents).

> TOOL

id: toolu_01JVddGVhkaYDK1uamW8dWqd
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/persisted/Projects/Sandcastle/images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh",
  "old_string": "# Manage the tmux auto-attach block in ~/.bashrc. Runs every container\n# start so toggling the setting applies on next sandbox launch. Uses\n# fenced markers so we can reliably replace/remove just our block without\n# disturbing user edits elsewhere in .bashrc.\nBASHRC=\"/home/$USERNAME/.bashrc\"\nBEGIN_MARK=\"# >>> sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach >>>\"\nEND_MARK=\"# <<< sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach <<<\"\nif [ -f \"$BASHRC\" ] || [ -f /etc/skel/.bashrc ]; then\n    [ -f \"$BASHRC\" ] || cp /etc/skel/.bashrc \"$BASHRC\" 2>/dev/null || touch \"$BASHRC\"\n    # Strip any previous block.\n    sed -i \"/^${BEGIN_MARK}$/,/^${END_MARK}$/d\" \"$BASHRC\" 2>/dev/null || true\n    if [ \"${SANDCASTLE_SSH_START_TMUX:-1}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n        cat >> \"$BASHRC\" <<'TMUX_EOF'\n# >>> sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach >>>\n# Auto-attach to a shared tmux session for interactive SSH logins.\n# Toggle per-sandbox via the \"Start tmux on SSH\" setting.\n# Bypass for a single connection with: ssh -o SetEnv=NO_TMUX=1 ...\nif [ -z \"$TMUX\" ] && [ -z \"$NO_TMUX\" ] && [ -n \"$SSH_CONNECTION\" ] && [[ $- == *i* ]] && command -v tmux >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    exec tmux new-session -A -s main\nfi\n# <<< sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach <<<\nTMUX_EOF\n    fi\n    chown \"$USERNAME:$USERNAME\" \"$BASHRC\" 2>/dev/null || true\nfi",
  "new_string": "# Manage the tmux auto-attach snippet in /etc/profile.d. Runs every\n# container start so toggling the setting applies on next sandbox launch.\n# Using /etc/profile.d (not ~/.bashrc) means the block fires reliably on\n# SSH login even when the user's home is a bind-mounted volume that\n# lacks a ~/.profile to source ~/.bashrc.\n#\n# Also tidy up the legacy ~/.bashrc block from earlier versions so it\n# can't double-exec or leave stale content behind.\nTMUX_PROFILE=/etc/profile.d/sandcastle-tmux.sh\nif [ \"${SANDCASTLE_SSH_START_TMUX:-1}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n    cat > \"$TMUX_PROFILE\" <<'TMUX_EOF'\n# Auto-attach to a shared tmux session for interactive SSH logins.\n# Toggle per-sandbox via the \"Start tmux on SSH\" setting.\n# Bypass for a single connection with: ssh -o SetEnv=NO_TMUX=1 ...\nif [ -z \"$TMUX\" ] && [ -z \"$NO_TMUX\" ] && [ -n \"$SSH_CONNECTION\" ] && [ -t 0 ] && command -v tmux >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    exec tmux new-session -A -s main\nfi\nTMUX_EOF\n    chmod 0644 \"$TMUX_PROFILE\"\nelse\n    rm -f \"$TMUX_PROFILE\"\nfi\n\n# Remove legacy ~/.bashrc block from prior versions (pre /etc/profile.d move).\nBASHRC=\"/home/$USERNAME/.bashrc\"\nif [ -f \"$BASHRC\" ]; then\n    sed -i '/^# >>> sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach >>>$/,/^# <<< sandcastle ssh tmux auto-attach <<<$/d' \"$BASHRC\" 2>/dev/null || true\nfi"
}
```

{
  "command": "git add images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh && git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfix(sandbox): move SSH tmux auto-attach to /etc/profile.d\n\nThe auto-attach block was appended to ~/.bashrc, which is only sourced\non SSH login when ~/.profile exists and sources it. For sandboxes with\nmount_home=true, the bind-mounted home is empty on first launch and\nuseradd -m does not seed /etc/skel (directory already exists), so\n~/.profile is missing and the tmux block never runs. SSH logins land\nin plain bash regardless of the \"Start tmux on SSH\" setting.\n\nWrite to /etc/profile.d/sandcastle-tmux.sh on every container start\ninstead — always sourced by login shells, independent of the user's\nhome contents. Also drop the legacy ~/.bashrc block from earlier\nversions so stale content doesn't double-exec tmux.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit tmux-on-SSH fix"
}

> AGENT

Fixed, committed `83ec1bd`, and tagged/pushed `v0.8.103`.

**What was wrong:** the tmux auto-attach was appended to `~/.bashrc`. For sandboxes with `mount_home=true`, the bind-mounted home is empty on first launch and `useradd -m` doesn't re-seed `/etc/skel`, so there's no `~/.profile` and SSH login shells never source `~/.bashrc`. The block never ran — SSH landed in plain bash.

**Fix:** write `/etc/profile.d/sandcastle-tmux.sh` on every container start instead. `/etc/profile.d/*.sh` is always sourced by login shells, independent of home contents. Also strips the legacy `~/.bashrc` block so existing sandboxes with stale content don't double-exec.

**Verification required:** after GitHub Actions rebuilds the sandbox image, the existing sandbox needs a full stop → start (which recreates the container with the new image) to pick up the fix. Then `ssh user@host` should land directly in a tmux `main` session.
